- **codebase-map-reviewer**: Reviews the codebase, route and design maps against the code for missing modules, unmapped routes and stale entries. Dispatched by the Lead per codebase in Phase -1B as a tier-3 panel of three via ct6-converge. Read-only; JSON verdict. ([paulingram/claude-skills](https://github.com/paulingram/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/codebase-map-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **codebase-mapper**: Use proactively when a task needs local repository structure, entry points, ownership, data flow, or change-surface mapping before a decision. Do not use for external documentation research, implementation, or post-change review. ([zebbern/claude-code-guide](https://github.com/zebbern/claude-code-guide/blob/HEAD/agents/.claude/agents/codebase-mapper.md) · ★4.6k)
